Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on ultrasound structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 538. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 403 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 538 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ultrasound structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 403. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the ultrasound structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 853. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 217 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the ultrasound space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 853 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 217. QED.
